- **NEW defect issue (create_issue this run)**: crossterm delivered-resize race. Mechanism: in crossterm 0.29's Unix event source a SIGWINCH and a key landing in the same ~16 ms epoll batch return the key without draining the signal pipe; mio's edge-triggered epoll then never re-reports the byte, so the layout lags until the next actual size change. Tester evidence: 4/6 bounded reproductions (kernel winsize 100x40 vs rendered 62x35) plus strace (`read(0, "\r")` returned, then epoll timeouts with the signal byte unread); all lost cases recovered on the next resize. Pre-existing on main; the #631 vehicle is test-only. Body carries the per-frame `crossterm::terminal::size()` reconciliation fix direction anchored at `main.rs:549,596-606`. UNTRIAGED in STATE; self-triage expected to route `build`.
- **#612 CLOSED** 00:53:44Z with full evidence links (sibling 39084280973, independently re-verified by 39084348167). DONE.
